- **blast-radius**: Finds what a change breaks outside its own diff, and proves the one fact it is safe because of by running code instead of writing it up. Reads what grep cannot reach, such as a pinned library's source, a wire format, or a caller three hops downstream. Use for "what could this break", "blast radius of X", a small diff you do not trust, or before merging a change to something shared. For a full review, use `prod-review`. ([nk-o/oner](https://github.com/nk-o/oner/tree/HEAD/skills/blast-radius))
- **blast-radius**: Use this skill whenever the user asks what a change could break, its blast radius, whether a rename or small diff is really safe, or says they do not trust a small change, including a pasted diff with no repo or shell. Do not answer such a question from the diff alone. This skill looks where grep stops (wire formats, other languages reading the same data, timing, library internals), names the one fact the change is safe because of, proves it by running real code or marks it unproven, and separates confirmed risks from cleared ones with the cheapest check to run before merge.
